# Rust crates in Glint

Glint (`Glint.exe` and the instant search service, `shadui-index.exe`) is built from these crates from [crates.io](https://crates.io), each under its own license, whose text follows with the crates it covers. The programs Glint ships beside it (ripgrep, fd and pdu), and the notices some of these crates ask for, are in [THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md](THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md).

This file is made by [cargo-about](https://github.com/EmbarkStudios/cargo-about) from `src-tauri/Cargo.lock` with `src-tauri/about.toml` and `src-tauri/about.hbs`: `npm run licenses` writes it again.

| License | Crates |
| :--- | ---: |
| MIT License | 557 |
| ISC License | 24 |
| Unicode License v3 | 20 |
| Apache License 2.0 | 16 |
| BSD 3-Clause "New" or "Revised" License | 15 |
| Mozilla Public License 2.0 | 6 |
| MIT No Attribution | 4 |
| zlib License | 4 |
| BSD 2-Clause "Simplified" License | 3 |
| Creative Commons Zero v1.0 Universal | 2 |
| Boost Software License 1.0 | 1 |
| Community Data License Agreement Permissive 2.0 | 1 |
| Unicode License Agreement - Data Files and Software (2016) | 1 |
| bzip2 and libbzip2 License v1.0.6 | 1 |

````text
/// The PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_PUA: [u16; 18] = [
    0xE78D, 0xE78E, 0xE78F, 0xE790, 0xE791, 0xE792, 0xE793, 0xE794, 0xE795, 0xE796, 0xE81E, 0xE826,
    0xE82B, 0xE82C, 0xE832, 0xE843, 0xE854, 0xE864,
];

/// The bytes corresponding to the PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_BYTES: [[u8; 2]; 18] = [
    [0xA6, 0xD9],
    [0xA6, 0xDA],
    [0xA6, 0xDB],
    [0xA6, 0xDC],
    [0xA6, 0xDD],
    [0xA6, 0xDE],
    [0xA6, 0xDF],
    [0xA6, 0xEC],
    [0xA6, 0xED],
    [0xA6, 0xF3],
    [0xFE, 0x59],
    [0xFE, 0x61],
    [0xFE, 0x66],
    [0xFE, 0x67],
    [0xFE, 0x6D],
    [0xFE, 0x7E],
    [0xFE, 0x90],
    [0xFE, 0xA0],
];

````

````text
#define P384_LIMBS (384u / LIMB_BITS)

} P384_POINT;

} P384_POINT_AFFINE;
````

````text
//! EdDSA Signatures.

use super::ops::ELEM_LEN;
use crate::digest;

pub mod signing;
pub mod verification;

/// The length of an Ed25519 public key.
pub const ED25519_PUBLIC_KEY_LEN: usize = ELEM_LEN;

pub fn eddsa_digest(signature_r: &[u8], public_key: &[u8], msg: &[u8]) -> digest::Digest {
    let mut ctx = digest::Context::new(&digest::SHA512);
    ctx.update(signature_r);
    ctx.update(public_key);
    ctx.update(msg);
    ctx.finish()
}

````

````text
use crate::limb::Limb;

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
#[repr(transparent)]
pub struct N0([Limb; 2]);

impl N0 {
    #[cfg(feature = "alloc")]
    pub(super) const LIMBS_USED: usize = 64 / crate::limb::LIMB_BITS;

    #[inline]
    pub const fn precalculated(n0: u64) -> Self {
        #[cfg(target_pointer_width = "64")]
        {
            Self([n0, 0])
        }

        #[cfg(target_pointer_width = "32")]
        {
            Self([n0 as Limb, (n0 >> crate::limb::LIMB_BITS) as Limb])
        }
    }
}

````

## ISC License

[ring 0.17.14](https://github.com/briansmith/ring)

````text
// Copyright 2018 Brian Smith.
//
// Permission to use, copy, modify, and/or distribute this software for any
// purpose with or without fee is hereby granted, provided that the above
// copyright notice and this permission notice appear in all copies.
//
// THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS" AND THE AUTHOR DISCLAIMS ALL WARRANTIES
// WITH REGARD TO THIS SOFTWARE INCLUDING ALL IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF
// MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE AUTHOR BE LIABLE FOR ANY
// SPECIAL, DIRECT, INDIRECT, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES OR ANY DAMAGES
// WHATSOEVER RESULTING FROM LOSS OF USE, DATA OR PROFITS, WHETHER IN AN ACTION
// OF CONTRACT, NEGLIGENCE OR OTHER TORTIOUS ACTION, ARISING OUT OF OR IN
// CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.

#[cfg(target_arch = "x86")]
pub fn shift_full_blocks<const BLOCK_LEN: usize>(
    in_out: super::overlapping::Overlapping<'_, u8>,
    mut transform: impl FnMut(&[u8; BLOCK_LEN]) -> [u8; BLOCK_LEN],
) {
    let (in_out, src) = in_out.into_slice_src_mut();
    let in_out_len = in_out[src.clone()].len();

    for i in (0..in_out_len).step_by(BLOCK_LEN) {
        let block = {
            let input =
                <&[u8; BLOCK_LEN]>::try_from(&in_out[(src.start + i)..][..BLOCK_LEN]).unwrap();
            transform(input)
        };
        let output = <&mut [u8; BLOCK_LEN]>::try_from(&mut in_out[i..][..BLOCK_LEN]).unwrap();
        *output = block;
    }
}

````

````text
/// Polyfill for `Option::unwrap()` as a const fn; feature `const_option`.
/// https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/67441.
/// TODO(MSRV): Replace this with `x.unwrap()`.
///
/// `T: Copy` avoids "constant functions cannot evaluate destructors."
pub const fn unwrap_const<T>(x: Option<T>) -> T
where
    T: Copy,
{
    if let Some(x) = x {
        x
    } else {
        panic!("unwrap_const on `None`");
    }
}

````

````text
// CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.

//! Utilities to make dealing with slices less tediuous.

/// Replaces the first N elements of `a` with the first N elements of `b`, where
/// N is `core::cmp::min(a.len(), b.len())`, leaving the rest unchanged.
pub fn overwrite_at_start<T: Copy>(a: &mut [T], b: &[T]) {
    a.iter_mut().zip(b).for_each(|(a, b)| {
        *a = *b;
    });
}

````

````text
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub struct NotSend(PhantomData<*mut ()>);

impl NotSend {
    pub const VALUE: Self = Self(PhantomData);
}

#[allow(deprecated)]
const _: () = testutil::compile_time_assert_clone::<NotSend>();
#[allow(deprecated)]
const _: () = testutil::compile_time_assert_copy::<NotSend>();
const _: () = assert!(size_of::<NotSend>() == 0);

````

## MIT License

[schemars_derive 0.8.22](https://github.com/GREsau/schemars)

````text
#![allow(clippy::all)]
// Copied from regex_syntax crate to avoid pulling in the whole crate just for a utility function
// https://github.com/rust-lang/regex/blob/431c4e4867e1eb33eb39b23ed47c9934b2672f8f/regex-syntax/src/lib.rs
//
// Copyright (c) 2014 The Rust Project Developers
//
// Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any
// person obtaining a copy of this software and associated
// documentation files (the "Software"), to deal in the
// Software without restriction, including without
// limitation the rights to use, copy, modify, merge,
// publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell copies of
// the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software
// is furnished to do so, subject to the following
// conditions:
//
// The above copyright notice and this permission notice
// shall be included in all copies or substantial portions
// of the Software.
//
// THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF
// ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED
// TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A
// PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT
// SHALL THE AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY
// CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION
// OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM, OUT OF OR
// IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER
// DEALINGS IN THE SOFTWARE.

pub fn escape(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut quoted = String::new();
    escape_into(text, &mut quoted);
    quoted
}

fn escape_into(text: &str, buf: &mut String) {
    buf.reserve(text.len());
    for c in text.chars() {
        if is_meta_character(c) {
            buf.push('\\');
        }
        buf.push(c);
    }
}

fn is_meta_character(c: char) -> bool {
    match c {
        '\\' | '.' | '+' | '*' | '?' | '(' | ')' | '|' | '[' | ']' | '{' | '}' | '^' | '$'
        | '#' | '&' | '-' | '~' => true,
        _ => false,
    }
}

````

<!-- embedded-assets: scripts/lib/embedded-notices.mjs writes everything below this line -->

# Fonts, icons and data embedded in the app

cargo-about lists the crates above by their own licenses. These crates also carry fonts, icons and data that are not theirs, under licenses of their own; they ship inside Glint.exe, so their notices are reproduced here. (Written by `npm run licenses`, from the crates' sources at the versions in Cargo.lock.)

## KaTeX fonts

The math in Markdown, Typst and LaTeX previews is drawn with the KaTeX fonts, embedded in Glint.exe by ratex-svg (`embed-fonts`, through ratex-katex-fonts) under the SIL Open Font License 1.1.

KaTeX font files (KaTeX_*.ttf)
================================

These TrueType fonts are taken from the KaTeX project:
  https://github.com/KaTeX/KaTeX

They are licensed under the SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1. The full
license text is in OFL.txt in this directory.

KaTeX's own MIT license applies to its software, not to the font binaries;
font licensing follows OFL as described in KaTeX's documentation and
upstream repository.

The fonts' own copyright notices:

- Copyright (c) 2009-2010 Design Science, Inc.; Copyright (c) 2014-2018 Khan Academy


## Typst's assets

Typst documents are laid out and exported to PDF with data typst-assets embeds: the PDF standard fonts (Foxit), ICC colour profiles, ICU line-breaking data and tables derived from the W3C's HTML and MathML specifications. Its bundled text fonts (Libertinus Serif, New Computer Modern, DejaVu) are not compiled in: Typst reads the PC's own fonts. The crate's notice, in full:

`````text
Licenses for third party components bundled by this project can be found below.
`````

## Citation styles and locales (hayagriva)

Typst's bibliographies use the Citation Style Language styles and locales hayagriva embeds, under the Creative Commons Attribution-ShareAlike 3.0 license (https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/3.0/). The styles are from https://github.com/citation-style-language/styles and the locales from https://github.com/citation-style-language/locales; each file names its authors. The crate's notice:

`````text
Licenses for third party components used by this project can be found below.

================================================================================
The Creative Commons BY-SA 3.0 DEED License applies to:
* The CSL styles found in `tests/data/*`
* The CSL styles and locales found in `archive/`

https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/3.0/
`````

## Hyphenation patterns (hypher)

Typst hyphenates with the patterns hypher embeds. Its README says of them:

`````text
## License
The code of this crate is dual-licensed under the MIT and Apache 2.0 licenses.

The files in `patterns/` are subject to the individual licenses stated therein.
The patterns are processed at build time and then embedded (i.e. statically
linked) into your binary. However, _hypher_ includes only patterns that are
available under permissive licenses. Patterns licenses include the LPPL, MPL,
MIT, BSD-3.
`````

## Time zone database (jiff-tzdb)

Dates in time zones use the IANA time zone database jiff-tzdb embeds, which is in the public domain (https://www.iana.org/time-zones). jiff-tzdb's own code is dual-licensed under the Unlicense and MIT (listed above).
